===== FILE: /opt/oci-zero-cost-lab/panel/server.py =====
from __future__ import annotations
import json, os, secrets, traceback, mimetypes
from http.server import BaseHTTPRequestHandler, ThreadingHTTPServer
from urllib.parse import urlparse, parse_qs
from . import common, auth, system, singbox, nodes, security, logs, ai, learning, templates, certificates, system_monitor, system_updates
from .raw_subscription import raw_nodes

STATIC_ROOT = os.path.abspath(
    os.path.join(os.path.dirname(__file__), "static")
)

def cookies(h):
    out={}
    for p in h.get("Cookie","").split(";"):
        if "=" in p:
            k,v=p.strip().split("=",1); out[k]=v
    return out

def static_file(path):
    """
    Serve authenticated panel static assets.

    Security:
    - Only files under panel/static are allowed.
    - Path traversal is rejected.
    - Directory access is rejected.
    """
    rel = path[len("/static/"):]
    if not rel:
        return None

    # URL path -> filesystem path
    candidate = os.path.abspath(
        os.path.join(STATIC_ROOT, rel)
    )

    # Prevent path traversal.
    try:
        if os.path.commonpath([STATIC_ROOT, candidate]) != STATIC_ROOT:
            return None
    except ValueError:
        return None

    if not os.path.isfile(candidate):
        return None

    try:
        with open(candidate, "rb") as f:
            data = f.read()
    except OSError:
        return None

    ctype = mimetypes.guess_type(candidate)[0]

    if not ctype:
        if candidate.endswith(".js"):
            ctype = "text/javascript; charset=utf-8"
        elif candidate.endswith(".css"):
            ctype = "text/css; charset=utf-8"
        else:
            ctype = "application/octet-stream"

    return data, ctype


class H(BaseHTTPRequestHandler):
    server_version="OCI-Linux-Lab/2.0"
    def log_message(self,*a): pass
    def json(self,obj,code=200):
        b=json.dumps(obj,ensure_ascii=False).encode()
        self.send_response(code); self.send_header("Content-Type","application/json; charset=utf-8")
        self.send_header("Content-Length",str(len(b))); self.send_header("X-Content-Type-Options","nosniff")
        self.end_headers(); self.wfile.write(b)
    def page(self,b,code=200,ctype="text/html; charset=utf-8"):
        self.send_response(code); self.send_header("Content-Type",ctype); self.send_header("Content-Length",str(len(b)))
        self.send_header("X-Content-Type-Options","nosniff"); self.send_header("X-Frame-Options","DENY")
        self.end_headers(); self.wfile.write(b)
    def body(self):
        n=int(self.headers.get("Content-Length","0")); return json.loads(self.rfile.read(n) or b"{}")
    def sid(self): return cookies(self.headers).get("oci_sid")
    def session(self): return auth.session(self.sid() or "")
    def need(self):
        if not self.session(): self.json({"error":"unauthorized"},401); return None
        return self.session()
    def send_sid(self,sid):
        self.send_header("Set-Cookie",f"oci_sid={sid}; Path=/; HttpOnly; Secure; SameSite=Strict")
    def do_GET(self):
        p=urlparse(self.path)

        # 浏览器自动请求 favicon。
        # 当前 Panel 没有 favicon 文件，因此明确返回 204，
        # 避免进入认证流程产生 401，也避免静态文件路由产生 404。
        if p.path == "/favicon.ico":
            self.send_response(204)
            self.end_headers()
            return
        if p.path=="/healthz": self.json({"ok":True,"service":"oci-linux-lab","version":"2.0.0-modular"}); return
        if p.path=="/login": self.page(templates.LOGIN.encode()); return
        if p.path=="/":
            if not self.session(): self.send_response(302); self.send_header("Location","/login"); self.end_headers(); return
            self.page(templates.HTML.encode()); return
        if p.path.startswith("/sub/"):
            tok=p.path.split("/",2)[2]; real=common.env_value("SUB_TOKEN") or common.env_value("SUBSCRIPTION_TOKEN") or common.env_value("CLASH_TOKEN") or common.env_value("PANEL_SUB_TOKEN")
            if not real or not secrets.compare_digest(tok,real): self.page(b"not found",404); return
            # Minimal valid Clash Meta YAML, generated without PyYAML.
            self.page(subscription_text().encode(),200,"text/yaml; charset=utf-8"); return
        # Static assets do not contain user/session data.
        # They must be available before the authenticated API/page routes,
        # otherwise the ES module frontend cannot bootstrap.
        if p.path.startswith("/static/"):
            result = static_file(p.path)
            if result is None:
                self.json({"error":"not found"},404)
                return

            data, ctype = result
            self.page(data,200,ctype)
            return

        if not self.need(): return

        try:
            if p.path=="/api/session":
                self.json({
                    "ok": True,
                    "authenticated": True,
                    "csrf": self.session().get("csrf","")
                })
            elif p.path=="/api/status":
                x = system.status()
                x["csrf"] = self.session().get("csrf","")
                self.json(x)
            elif p.path=="/api/singbox":
                self.json(singbox.config())
            elif p.path=="/api/nodes":
                self.json({"nodes":nodes.list_nodes()})
            elif p.path=="/api/subscription":
                x = subscription_info()
                x["raw_nodes"] = raw_nodes()
                self.json(x)
            elif p.path=="/api/security": self.json(security.status())
            elif p.path=="/api/certificates": self.json(certificates.status())
            elif p.path=="/api/system/monitor": self.json(system_monitor.collect())
            elif p.path=="/api/system/updates":
                q = parse_qs(p.query)
                component = q.get("component", [""])[0].strip().lower()
                force = q.get("refresh", ["0"])[0] == "1"

                if component:
                    self.json(
                        system_updates.check_component(
                            component,
                            force=force
                        )
                    )
                else:
                    self.json(
                        system_updates.collect(
                            force=force
                        )
                    )
            elif p.path=="/api/learning/courses": self.json({"courses":learning.courses()})
            elif p.path.startswith("/api/learning/lesson/"):
                cid=p.path.rsplit("/",1)[1]; c=learning.lesson(cid)
                self.json({"title":cid,"content":c or "not found"},200 if c else 404)
            elif p.path=="/api/learning/progress": self.json(learning.progress())
            elif p.path=="/api/services": self.json(system.services())
            elif p.path=="/api/ai/status": self.json(ai.status())
            elif p.path=="/api/logs": self.json({"log":logs.get(parse_qs(p.query).get("service",["oci-linux-lab"])[0])})
            else:self.json({"error":"not found"},404)
        except Exception as e:self.json({"error":str(e)},500)
    def do_POST(self):
        p=urlparse(self.path)
        if p.path=="/login":
            form=parse_qs(self.body_raw().decode(errors="replace"))
            u=form.get("user",[""])[0]; pw=form.get("password",[""])[0]; code=form.get("totp",[""])[0]
            if auth.password_ok(u,pw) and auth.verify_totp(code):
                sid,csrf=auth.new_session(); self.send_response(302); self.send_header("Location","/")
                self.send_sid(sid); self.end_headers(); return
            self.page(templates.LOGIN.replace("</form>","</form><p>登录失败</p>").encode(),401); return
        if p.path=="/logout":
            auth.logout(self.sid() or ""); self.send_response(204); self.end_headers(); return
        s=self.need()
        if not s:return
        try:
            d=self.body()
            if d.get("csrf") != s.get("csrf"):
                self.json({"error":"csrf validation failed"},403); return
            if p.path in ("/api/ai/chat","/api/learning/complete") and d.get("confirm") in (None,""):
                if p.path=="/api/ai/chat": self.json(ai.chat(d.get("messages",[])))
                else: self.json({"progress":learning.mark(str(d.get("id","")))})
                return
            if d.get("confirm") not in ("CONFIRM","CHANGE-PORT"):
                self.json({"error":"explicit confirmation required"},400); return
            if p.path=="/api/ai/chat": self.json(ai.chat(d.get("messages",[])))
            elif p.path=="/api/system/update":
                component = str(d.get("component", "")).strip().lower()

                if component not in (
                    "singbox",
                    "caddy",
                    "ollama",
                    "apt",
                ):
                    self.json(
                        {"error": "unknown update component"},
                        400
                    )
                    return

                self.json(
                    system_updates.update_component(
                        component
                    )
                )

            elif p.path=="/api/singbox/restart": self.json(singbox.restart())
            elif p.path=="/api/node/port": self.json(nodes.change_port(d.get("index"),d.get("port")))
            elif p.path=="/api/certificates/issue": self.json(certificates.issue())
            elif p.path=="/api/action":
                a=d.get("action")
                if a=="singbox-update": self.json(singbox.update())
                elif a=="system-update": self.json(system.update_system())
                else:self.json({"error":"unknown action"},400)
            else:self.json({"error":"not found"},404)
        except Exception as e:self.json({"error":str(e)},500)
    def body_raw(self):
        n=int(self.headers.get("Content-Length","0")); return self.rfile.read(n)

def subscription_info():
    d=common.parse_env(); tok=d.get("SUB_TOKEN") or d.get("SUBSCRIPTION_TOKEN") or d.get("CLASH_TOKEN") or d.get("PANEL_SUB_TOKEN")
    return {"host":common.public_host(),"token_present":bool(tok),"url":f"/sub/{tok}" if tok else ""}

def yamlq(s):
    return "'" + str(s).replace("'","''") + "'"

def subscription_text():
    cfg=common.read_json(common.SINGBOX,{})
    sec=common.parse_env(); host=common.public_host(); lines=["proxies:"]
    names=[]
    for x in cfg.get("inbounds",[]):
        typ=x.get("type")
        if typ not in ("vless","tuic","hysteria2"): continue
        tag=x.get("tag","node"); port=x.get("listen_port",x.get("port")); names.append(tag)
        users=x.get("users") or [{}]; u=users[0] if users else {}
        if typ=="vless":
            uuid=u.get("uuid") or sec.get("VLESS_REALITY_UUID") or sec.get("NODE_UUID","")
            tls=x.get("tls") or {}; reality=tls.get("reality") or {}
            lines += [f"  - name: {yamlq(tag)}", "    type: vless", f"    server: {yamlq(host)}",
                      f"    port: {port}",f"    uuid: {yamlq(uuid)}","    network: tcp","    tls: true"]
            if reality.get("enabled"):
                lines += [f"    servername: {yamlq(tls.get('server_name','www.microsoft.com'))}",
                          "    client-fingerprint: chrome",
                          f"    reality-opts:","      public-key: "+yamlq(sec.get("REALITY_PUBLIC","")),
                          f"        short-id: {yamlq(reality.get('short_id') or sec.get('REALITY_SHORT_ID',''))}"]
        elif typ=="tuic":
            uuid=u.get("uuid") or sec.get("TUIC_UUID","")
            pw=u.get("password") or sec.get("TUIC_PASSWORD","")
            tls=x.get("tls") or {}
            sni=tls.get("server_name") or host

            lines += [
                f"  - name: {yamlq(tag)}",
                "    type: tuic",
                f"    server: {yamlq(host)}",
                f"    port: {port}",
                f"    uuid: {yamlq(uuid)}",
                f"    password: {yamlq(pw)}",
                "    udp: true",
                "    congestion-controller: bbr",
                f"    sni: {yamlq(sni)}",
                "    alpn:",
                "      - h3"
            ]
        elif typ=="hysteria2":
            pw=u.get("password") or sec.get("HY2_PASSWORD",""); tls=x.get("tls") or {}
            lines += [f"  - name: {yamlq(tag)}","    type: hysteria2",f"    server: {yamlq(host)}",
                      f"    port: {port}",f"    password: {yamlq(pw)}"]
    lines += ["proxy-groups:","  - name: PROXY","    type: select","    proxies:"] + [f"      - {yamlq(n)}" for n in names]
    lines += ["rules:","  - MATCH,PROXY"]
    return "\n".join(lines)+"\n"

# OCI_SYSTEM_MONITOR_SERVER_PATCH_V1

def main():
    bind=os.environ.get("PANEL_BIND","127.0.0.1"); port=int(os.environ.get("PANEL_PORT","3000"))
    print(f"OCI Linux Lab listening on {bind}:{port}",flush=True)
    ThreadingHTTPServer((bind,port),H).serve_forever()
if __name__=="__main__": main()

===== FILE: /opt/oci-zero-cost-lab/panel/system_monitor.py =====
from __future__ import annotations

import subprocess
from datetime import datetime, timezone

MONITORED_SERVICES = {
    "sing-box": "sing-box.service",
    "Caddy": "caddy.service",
    "Panel": "oci-linux-lab.service",
    "Ollama": "ollama.service",
}

ERROR_SERVICES = {
    "sing-box": "sing-box.service",
    "Caddy": "caddy.service",
    "Ollama": "ollama.service",
    "Panel": "oci-linux-lab.service",
}

CURRENT_ERROR_MINUTES = 10
RECENT_ERROR_MINUTES = 60


def _run(cmd, timeout=15):
    try:
        p = subprocess.run(
            cmd,
            text=True,
            stdout=subprocess.PIPE,
            stderr=subprocess.STDOUT,
            timeout=timeout,
            check=False,
        )
        return p.returncode, (p.stdout or "").strip()
    except Exception as e:
        return 999, str(e)


def _service_active(unit):
    rc, out = _run(["systemctl", "is-active", unit], timeout=5)
    return rc == 0 and out.strip() == "active"


def _failed_units():
    rc, out = _run(
        ["systemctl", "--failed", "--no-legend", "--no-pager"],
        timeout=8,
    )
    if rc not in (0, 1):
        return []
    rows = []
    for line in out.splitlines():
        line = line.strip()
        if not line:
            continue
        parts = line.split(None, 4)
        if parts:
            rows.append({
                "unit": parts[0],
                "state": parts[2] if len(parts) > 2 else "",
                "sub": parts[3] if len(parts) > 3 else "",
                "description": parts[4] if len(parts) > 4 else "",
            })
    return rows


def _journal_errors(unit, minutes, limit=20):
    rc, out = _run(
        [
            "journalctl",
            "-u",
            unit,
            "-p",
            "err..alert",
            "--since",
            f"{minutes} minutes ago",
            "--no-pager",
            "-o",
            "short-iso",
            "-n",
            str(limit),
        ],
        timeout=12,
    )
    if rc not in (0, 1):
        return []
    lines = [x.strip() for x in out.splitlines() if x.strip()]
    return [
        x for x in lines
        if "No entries" not in x
        and "Journal begins at" not in x
        and "Hint:" not in x
    ]


def collect():
    down = []
    for label, unit in MONITORED_SERVICES.items():
        if not _service_active(unit):
            rc, state = _run(["systemctl", "is-active", unit], timeout=5)
            down.append({
                "name": label,
                "unit": unit,
                "state": state.strip() or f"exit={rc}",
            })

    failed_units = _failed_units()
    current_errors = {}
    recent = []

    for label, unit in ERROR_SERVICES.items():
        cur = _journal_errors(unit, CURRENT_ERROR_MINUTES, 8)
        if cur:
            current_errors[label] = {
                "unit": unit,
                "window_minutes": CURRENT_ERROR_MINUTES,
                "items": cur,
            }

        rec = _journal_errors(unit, RECENT_ERROR_MINUTES, 8)
        for line in rec:
            recent.append({"service": label, "line": line})

    seen = set()
    recent_unique = []
    for item in reversed(recent):
        key = (item["service"], item["line"])
        if key in seen:
            continue
        seen.add(key)
        recent_unique.append(item)
        if len(recent_unique) >= 30:
            break
    recent_unique.reverse()

    return {
        "ok": True,
        "generated_at": datetime.now(timezone.utc).astimezone().isoformat(timespec="seconds"),
        "current_window_minutes": CURRENT_ERROR_MINUTES,
        "recent_window_minutes": RECENT_ERROR_MINUTES,
        "service_down": down,
        "failed_units": failed_units,
        "current_errors": current_errors,
        "recent_errors": recent_unique,
        "has_issue": bool(down or failed_units or current_errors or recent_unique),
    }

===== FILE: /opt/oci-zero-cost-lab/panel/system_updates.py =====
from __future__ import annotations

import json
import os
import re
import shutil
import subprocess
import time
import urllib.request


# ============================================================================
# OCI Linux Lab
# 独立软件更新模块
#
# 每个组件完全独立检查：
#
#   singbox
#   caddy
#   ollama
#   apt
#
# 默认：
#   24 小时缓存
#   只检查
#   不自动更新
#
# 手动更新必须由 server.py 经过显式确认后调用 update_component()
# ============================================================================

CACHE_SECONDS = 86400

_CACHE = {}
_CACHE_TIME = {}

GITHUB_REPOS = {
    "singbox": "SagerNet/sing-box",
    "caddy": "caddyserver/caddy",
    "ollama": "ollama/ollama",
}


def _run(cmd, timeout=30):
    try:
        p = subprocess.run(
            cmd,
            text=True,
            stdout=subprocess.PIPE,
            stderr=subprocess.STDOUT,
            timeout=timeout,
            check=False,
        )

        return p.returncode, (p.stdout or "").strip()

    except Exception as e:
        return 999, str(e)


def _github_latest(repo):
    url = f"https://api.github.com/repos/{repo}/releases/latest"

    req = urllib.request.Request(
        url,
        headers={
            "Accept": "application/vnd.github+json",
            "User-Agent": "OCI-Linux-Lab-System-Update-Checker",
        },
    )

    try:
        with urllib.request.urlopen(req, timeout=12) as r:
            data = json.loads(r.read().decode("utf-8"))

        tag = str(
            data.get("tag_name") or ""
        ).strip()

        if tag.startswith("v"):
            tag = tag[1:]

        return {
            "version": tag,
            "url": data.get("html_url") or "",
            "published_at": data.get("published_at") or "",
            "error": "",
        }

    except Exception as e:
        return {
            "version": "",
            "url": f"https://github.com/{repo}/releases/latest",
            "published_at": "",
            "error": str(e),
        }


def _version_parts(v):
    s = str(v or "").strip().lower()

    if s.startswith("v"):
        s = s[1:]

    parts = re.findall(
        r"\d+|[a-z]+",
        s
    )

    out = []

    for p in parts:
        if p.isdigit():
            out.append((0, int(p)))
        else:
            out.append((1, p))

    return out


def _cmp(a, b):
    pa = _version_parts(a)
    pb = _version_parts(b)

    n = max(len(pa), len(pb))

    for i in range(n):

        xa = pa[i] if i < len(pa) else (0, 0)
        xb = pb[i] if i < len(pb) else (0, 0)

        if xa == xb:
            continue

        if xa[0] != xb[0]:
            return -1 if xa[0] < xb[0] else 1

        return -1 if xa[1] < xb[1] else 1

    return 0


def _newer(current, latest):
    return bool(
        current
        and latest
        and _cmp(latest, current) > 0
    )


def _singbox_version():

    path = (
        shutil.which("sing-box")
        or "/usr/bin/sing-box"
    )

    if not os.path.isfile(path):
        return {
            "current": "",
            "path": path,
            "error": "sing-box 未安装或不存在",
        }

    rc, out = _run(
        [path, "version"],
        timeout=8
    )

    m = re.search(
        r"(?:sing-box\s+)?version\s+v?([0-9][0-9A-Za-z.\-_]*)",
        out,
        re.I
    )

    if not m:
        m = re.search(
            r"\bv?([0-9]+\.[0-9]+(?:\.[0-9]+)?)\b",
            out
        )

    current = m.group(1) if m else ""

    return {
        "current": current,
        "path": path,
        "error": (
            ""
            if rc == 0 and current
            else (
                out
                or f"sing-box version check failed: exit={rc}"
            )
        ),
    }


def _caddy_version():

    path = (
        shutil.which("caddy")
        or "/usr/bin/caddy"
    )

    if not os.path.isfile(path):
        return {
            "current": "",
            "path": path,
            "error": "Caddy 未安装或不存在",
        }

    rc, out = _run(
        [path, "version"],
        timeout=8
    )

    m = re.search(
        r"v?([0-9]+\.[0-9]+\.[0-9]+[^\s]*)",
        out
    )

    current = m.group(1) if m else ""

    return {
        "current": current,
        "path": path,
        "error": (
            ""
            if rc == 0 and current
            else (
                out
                or f"Caddy version check failed: exit={rc}"
            )
        ),
    }


def _ollama_version():

    path = (
        shutil.which("ollama")
        or "/usr/local/bin/ollama"
    )

    # Ollama 已经作为 systemd 服务运行时，
    # 优先查询本机 API。
    #
    # 这样不会受到 Panel systemd 环境中 HOME
    # 未定义导致的 Ollama CLI panic 影响。

    try:
        req = urllib.request.Request(
            "http://127.0.0.1:11434/api/version", 
            headers={ 
                "User-Agent": "OCI-Linux-Lab-System-Update-Checker", 
            }, 
        ) 
 
        with urllib.request.urlopen( 
            req, 
            timeout=5 
        ) as r: 
 
            data = json.loads( 
                r.read().decode("utf-8") 
            ) 
 
        current = str( 
            data.get("version") or "" 
        ).strip() 
 
        if current: 
            return { 
                "current": current, 
                "path": path, 
                "error": "", 
            } 
 
    except Exception as e: 
 
        api_error = str(e) 
 
    else: 
        api_error = "Ollama API 未返回版本号" 
 
    # API 不可用时再回退到 CLI。 
    # CLI 必须提供有效 HOME。 
    # 
    # 当前服务器的 Ollama systemd 用户为： 
    #   ollama 
    # HOME： 
    #   /usr/share/ollama 
 
    rc, out = _run( 
        [ 
            "env", 
            "HOME=/usr/share/ollama", 
            "USER=ollama", 
            "LOGNAME=ollama", 
            path, 
            "--version", 
        ], 
        timeout=8 
    ) 
 
    m = re.search( 
        r"(?:ollama(?:\s+version)?\s*(?:is)?\s*)v?([0-9]+\.[0-9]+\.[0-9]+[^\s]*)", 
        out, 
        re.I 
    ) 
 
    if not m: 
        m = re.search( 
            r"\bv?([0-9]+\.[0-9]+\.[0-9]+[^\s]*)\b", 
            out 
        ) 
 
    current = m.group(1) if m else "" 
 
    return { 
        "current": current, 
        "path": path, 
        "error": ( 
            "" 
            if rc == 0 and current 
            else ( 
                out 
                or api_error 
                or f"Ollama version check failed: exit={rc}" 
            ) 
        ), 
    } 
 
 
def _ubuntu_apt(): 
 
    rc, osr = _run( 
        [ 
            "bash", 
            "-lc", 
            ". /etc/os-release 2>/dev/null; " 
            "echo ${PRETTY_NAME:-unknown}" 
        ], 
        timeout=5 
    ) 
 
    ubuntu = osr.strip() if rc == 0 else "unknown" 
 
    update_rc, update_out = _run( 
        [ 
            "apt-get", 
            "update", 
            "-qq" 
        ], 
        timeout=120 
    ) 
 
    rc2, sim = _run( 
        [ 
            "apt-get", 
            "-s", 
            "-o", 
            "APT::Get::Show-Upgraded=true", 
            "upgrade" 
        ], 
        timeout=60 
    ) 
 
    packages = [] 
    security = 0 
 
    if rc2 == 0: 
 
        for line in sim.splitlines(): 
 
            line = line.strip() 
 
            if not line.startswith("Inst "): 
                continue 
 
            parts = line.split() 
 
            if len(parts) < 2: 
                continue 
 
            packages.append(parts[1]) 
 
            if ( 
                "security.ubuntu.com" in line 
                or "jammy-security" in line 
                or "-security" in line 
            ): 
                security += 1 
 
    return { 
        "ubuntu": ubuntu, 
        "available_packages": len(packages), 
        "security_packages": security, 
        "packages": packages[:200], 
        "update_index_ok": update_rc == 0, 
        "simulation_ok": rc2 == 0, 
        "error": ( 
            "" 
            if update_rc == 0 and rc2 == 0 
            else ( 
                update_out 
                or sim 
                or f"apt exit={update_rc}/{rc2}" 
            ) 
        ), 
    } 
 
 
def _component(name, current_info, latest_info): 
 
    current = current_info.get("current", "") 
    latest = latest_info.get("version", "") 
 
    err = ( 
        current_info.get("error") 
        or latest_info.get("error") 
        or "" 
    ) 
 
    newer = _newer(current, latest) 
 
    if newer: 
        status = "update_available" 
    elif current and latest: 
        status = "up_to_date" 
    elif err: 
        status = "check_failed" 
    else: 
        status = "unknown" 
 
    return { 
        "name": name, 
        "current": current, 
        "latest": latest, 
        "newer": newer, 
        "status": status, 
        "release_url": latest_info.get("url", ""), 
        "published_at": latest_info.get("published_at", ""), 
        "error": err, 
        "path": current_info.get("path", ""), 
    } 
 
 
# ============================================================================ 
# 单模块检查 
# ============================================================================ 
 
def check_component(component, force=False): 
 
    component = str(component or "").strip().lower() 
 
    if component not in ( 
        "singbox", 
        "caddy", 
        "ollama", 
        "apt", 
    ): 
        raise ValueError( 
            f"未知更新模块: {component}" 
        ) 
 
    now = time.time() 
 
    if ( 
        not force 
        and component in _CACHE 
        and now - _CACHE_TIME.get(component, 0) < CACHE_SECONDS 
    ): 
        return _CACHE[component] 
 
    if component == "singbox": 
 
        current = _singbox_version() 
        latest = _github_latest( 
            GITHUB_REPOS["singbox"] 
        ) 
 
        data = _component( 
            "sing-box", 
            current, 
            latest 
        ) 
 
    elif component == "caddy": 
 
        current = _caddy_version() 
        latest = _github_latest( 
            GITHUB_REPOS["caddy"] 
        ) 
 
        data = _component( 
            "Caddy", 
            current, 
            latest 
        ) 
 
    elif component == "ollama": 
 
        current = _ollama_version() 
        latest = _github_latest( 
            GITHUB_REPOS["ollama"] 
        ) 
 
        data = _component( 
            "Ollama", 
            current, 
            latest 
        ) 
 
    else: 
 
        data = _ubuntu_apt() 
 
    checked_at = time.strftime( 
        "%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%SZ", 
        time.gmtime(now) 
    ) 
 
    if component != "apt": 
        data["checked_at"] = checked_at 
 
    else: 
        data["checked_at"] = checked_at 
 
    _CACHE[component] = data 
    _CACHE_TIME[component] = now 
 
    return data 
 
 
# ============================================================================ 
# 保留总览接口 
# 
# 仅用于： 
#   首次进入页面 
#   每日自动检查 
# 
# 手动点击模块按钮不会调用这里。 
# ============================================================================

def collect(force=False):

    return {
        "ok": True,
        "checked_at": time.strftime(
            "%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%SZ",
            time.gmtime()
        ),
        "cache_seconds": CACHE_SECONDS,
        "singbox": check_component(
            "singbox",
            force=force
        ),
        "apt": check_component(
            "apt",
            force=force
        ),
        "caddy": check_component(
            "caddy",
            force=force
        ),
        "ollama": check_component(
            "ollama",
            force=force
        ),
    }


# ============================================================================
# 手动更新
#
# 注意：
#   本函数不会被 GET 调用。
#   server.py 必须先验证 CONFIRM，
#   然后才允许执行。
# ============================================================================

def update_component(component):

    component = str(component or "").strip().lower()

    if component == "singbox":

        rc, out = _run(
            [
                "systemd-run",
                "--wait",
                "--pipe",
                "--collect",
                "/opt/oci-zero-cost-lab/scripts/singbox-update",
            ],
            timeout=900
        )

    elif component == "apt":

        rc, out = _run(
            [
                "systemd-run",
                "--wait",
                "--pipe",
                "--collect",
                "/opt/oci-zero-cost-lab/scripts/system-update",
            ],
            timeout=900
        )

    elif component == "caddy":

        # 使用 Caddy 官方推荐安装脚本。
        # 不修改 Caddyfile。
        rc, out = _run(
            [
                "bash",
                "-lc",
                "curl -fsSL https://getcaddy.com "
                "| bash -s personal && "
                "systemctl restart caddy"
            ],
            timeout=900
        )

    elif component == "ollama":

        # 使用 Ollama 官方安装脚本。
        # 不删除模型目录。
        rc, out = _run(
            [
                "bash",
                "-lc",
                "curl -fsSL https://ollama.com/install.sh | sh && " 
                "systemctl restart ollama" 
            ], 
            timeout=900 
        ) 
 
    else: 
 
        return { 
            "ok": False, 
            "component": component, 
            "error": f"未知更新模块: {component}", 
        } 
 
    # 更新成功后清除对应缓存。 
    _CACHE.pop(component, None) 
    _CACHE_TIME.pop(component, None) 
 
    return { 
        "ok": rc == 0, 
        "component": component, 
        "output": out[-6000:], 
        "exit_code": rc, 
    }

import { api } from "../core/api.js"; 
import { esc } from "../core/ui.js"; 
import { ociMonitorStart } from "./overview-monitor.js"; 
 
export async function render() { 
 
    const app = document.querySelector("#app"); 
 
    if (!app) { 
        return; 
    } 
 
    const x = await api("/api/status"); 
 
    if (!x) { 
        return; 
    } 
 
    const memory = x.memory_mb || {}; 
    const disk = x.disk_gb || {}; 
    const services = x.services || {}; 
 
    app.innerHTML = ` 
        <h2>系统概览</h2> 
 
        <div class="grid"> 
 
            <div class="card"> 
                CPU 
                <br> 
                <b>${esc(x.load?.[0] ?? "-")}</b> 
            </div> 
 
            <div class="card"> 
                内存 
                <br> 
                <b> 
                    ${esc(memory.used ?? "-")} 
                    / 
                    ${esc(memory.total ?? "-")} 
                    MB 
                </b> 
            </div> 
 
            <div class="card"> 
                磁盘 
                <br> 
                <b> 
                    ${esc(disk.used ?? "-")} 
                    / 
                    ${esc(disk.total ?? "-")} 
                    GB 
                </b> 
            </div> 
 
            <div class="card"> 
                BBR 
                <br> 
                <b>${esc(x.bbr ?? "-")}</b> 
            </div> 
 
        </div> 
 
        <div class="card"> 
            <h3>服务状态</h3> 
 
            ${ 
                Object.entries(services) 
                    .map(([name, active]) => ` 
                        <div style="line-height:1.9"> 
                            ${esc(name)}： 
                            <span class="${active ? "ok" : "bad"}"> 
                                ${active ? "● active" : "○ inactive"} 
                            </span> 
                        </div> 
                    `) 
                    .join("") 
            } 
        </div> 
 
        <div class="card"> 
            <h3>防火墙</h3> 
            <pre>${esc(x.ufw ?? "未知")}</pre> 
        </div> 
 
        <div id="oci-system-monitor"> 
            <div class="card"> 
                <div class="loading"> 
                    正在读取系统监控…… 
                </div> 
            </div> 
        </div> 
    `; 
 
    ociMonitorStart(); 
} 
 
===== FILE: /opt/oci-zero-cost-lab/panel/static/modules/overview-monitor.js ===== 
import { api } from "../core/api.js"; 
import { esc } from "../core/ui.js"; 
 
let monitorTimer = null; 
 
const SERVICES = [ 
    { 
        key: "sing-box", 
        label: "sing-box", 
        unit: "sing-box.service" 
    }, 
    { 
        key: "caddy", 
        label: "Caddy", 
        unit: "caddy.service" 
    }, 
    { 
        key: "ollama", 
        label: "Ollama", 
        unit: "ollama.service" 
    }, 
    { 
        key: "oci-linux-lab", 
        label: "Panel", 
        unit: "oci-linux-lab.service" 
    } 
]; 
 
function serviceState(health, key) { 
 
    const down = Array.isArray(health.service_down) 
        ? health.service_down 
        : []; 
 
    return !down.includes(key); 
} 
 
function serviceError(health, key) { 
 
    const errors = health.current_errors; 
 
    if (!errors || typeof errors !== "object") { 
        return ""; 
    } 
 
    return errors[key] || ""; 
} 
 
function renderErrorText(value) { 
 
    if (!value) { 
        return ` 
            <span style="opacity:.65"> 
                当前无错误 
            </span> 
        `; 
    } 
 
    return ` 
        <details> 
            <summary style="cursor:pointer"> 
                查看错误 
            </summary> 
            <pre style=" 
                margin-top:8px; 
                white-space:pre-wrap; 
                word-break:break-word; 
                font-size:12px; 
                line-height:1.5; 
            ">${esc(value)}</pre> 
        </details> 
    `; 
} 
 
function renderRecentErrors(errors) { 
 
    if (!Array.isArray(errors) || errors.length === 0) { 
        return ` 
            <div style="opacity:.65"> 
                最近窗口内没有发现错误。 
            </div> 
        `; 
    } 
 
    return errors.map(item => { 
 
        if (typeof item === "string") { 
            return ` 
                <pre style=" 
                    white-space:pre-wrap; 
                    word-break:break-word; 
                    font-size:12px; 
                ">${esc(item)}</pre> 
            `; 
        } 
 
        const service = item.service ?? ""; 
        const timestamp = item.timestamp ?? ""; 
        const message = item.message ?? item.error ?? ""; 
 
        return ` 
            <div style=" 
                padding:8px 0; 
                border-bottom:1px solid rgba(127,127,127,.15); 
            "> 
                <div style="font-size:12px;opacity:.7"> 
                    ${esc(timestamp)} 
                    ${service ? " · " + esc(service) : ""} 
                </div> 
 
                <pre style=" 
                    margin:4px 0 0; 
                    white-space:pre-wrap; 
                    word-break:break-word; 
                    font-size:12px; 
                ">${esc(message)}</pre> 
            </div> 
        `; 
    }).join(""); 
} 
 
async function load() { 
 
    const box = document.querySelector("#oci-system-monitor"); 
 
    if (!box) { 
        return; 
    } 
 
    try { 
 
        const health = await api("/api/system/monitor"); 
 
        if (!health) { 
            return; 
        } 
 
        const statusOK = health.has_issue !== true; 
 
        const serviceDown = Array.isArray(health.service_down) 
            ? health.service_down 
            : []; 
 
        const failedUnits = Array.isArray(health.failed_units) 
            ? health.failed_units 
            : []; 
 
        const recentErrors = Array.isArray(health.recent_errors) 
            ? health.recent_errors 
            : []; 
 
        const generated = health.generated_at 
            ? new Date(health.generated_at).toLocaleString() 
            : "-"; 
 
        box.innerHTML = ` 
            <div class="card"> 
 
                <h3> 
                    系统监控 
                </h3> 
 
                <div style=" 
                    margin-bottom:16px; 
                    padding:12px; 
                    border-radius:10px; 
                    background:rgba(127,127,127,.08); 
                "> 
 
                    <div style=" 
                        display:flex; 
                        justify-content:space-between; 
                        align-items:center; 
                        gap:12px; 
                        flex-wrap:wrap; 
                    "> 
 
                        <div> 
                            <b>整体状态：</b> 
 
                            <span class="${statusOK ? "ok" : "bad"}"> 
                                ${statusOK ? "● 正常" : "● 发现异常"} 
                            </span> 
                        </div> 
 
                        <div style=" 
                            font-size:12px; 
                            opacity:.65; 
                        "> 
                            ${esc(generated)} 
                        </div> 
 
                    </div> 
 
                </div> 
 
                <div class="grid"> 
 
                    ${ 
                        SERVICES.map(service => { 
 
                            const active = 
                                !serviceDown.includes(service.key); 
 
                            const error = 
                                serviceError(health, service.key); 
 
                            return ` 
                                <div class="stat"> 
 
                                    <div class="stat-name"> 
                                        ${esc(service.label)} 
                                    </div> 
 
                                    <div class="stat-value"> 
 
                                        <span class="${active ? "ok" : "bad"}"> 
                                            ${active 
                                                ? "● 运行正常" 
                                                : "● 服务异常"} 
                                        </span> 
 
                                    </div> 
 
                                    <div style=" 
                                        margin-top:8px; 
                                        font-size:12px; 
                                    "> 
                                        ${renderErrorText(error)} 
                                    </div> 
 
                                </div> 
                            `; 
                        }).join("") 
                    } 
 
                </div> 
 
                <div style=" 
                    margin-top:16px; 
                    padding:12px; 
                    border-radius:10px; 
                    background:rgba(127,127,127,.08); 
                "> 
 
                    <div style=" 
                        font-weight:600; 
                        margin-bottom:8px; 
                    "> 
                        systemd failed units 
                    </div> 
 
                    ${ 
                        failedUnits.length 
                            ? ` 
                                <pre style=" 
                                    white-space:pre-wrap; 
                                    word-break:break-word; 
                                    font-size:12px; 
                                ">${esc(failedUnits.join("\n"))}</pre> 
                            ` 
                            : ` 
                                <span class="ok"> 
                                    ● 没有 failed units 
                                </span> 
                            ` 
                    } 
 
                </div> 
 
                <div style=" 
                    margin-top:16px; 
                    padding:12px; 
                    border-radius:10px; 
                    background:rgba(127,127,127,.08); 
                "> 
 
                    <div style=" 
                        font-weight:600; 
                        margin-bottom:8px; 
                    "> 
                        最近错误详情 
                    </div> 
 
                    <div style=" 
                        font-size:12px; 
                        opacity:.65; 
                        margin-bottom:10px; 
                    "> 
                        当前窗口： 
                        ${esc(health.current_window_minutes ?? "-")} 分钟 
                        / 
                        最近窗口： 
                        ${esc(health.recent_window_minutes ?? "-")} 分钟 
                    </div> 
 
                    ${renderRecentErrors(recentErrors)} 
 
                </div> 
 
            </div> 
        `; 
 
    } catch (error) { 
 
        console.error("system monitor:", error); 
 
        box.innerHTML = ` 
            <div class="card"> 
 
                <h3>系统监控</h3> 
 
                <div style="opacity:.7"> 
                    监控数据暂时无法读取。 
                </div> 
 
            </div> 
        `; 
    } 
} 
 
export function ociMonitorLoad() { 
    return load(); 
} 
 
export function ociMonitorStart() { 
 
    if (monitorTimer) { 
        clearInterval(monitorTimer); 
        monitorTimer = null; 
    } 
 
    load(); 
 
    /* 
     * 页面监控刷新： 
     * 30 秒一次。 
     * 
     * 后端本身不产生后台更新动作， 
     * 这里只读取状态。 
     */ 
    monitorTimer = setInterval(() => { 
 
        if (document.querySelector("#oci-system-monitor")) { 
            load(); 
        } else { 
            clearInterval(monitorTimer); 
            monitorTimer = null; 
        } 
 
    }, 30000); 
}

===== FILE: /opt/oci-zero-cost-lab/panel/static/modules/singbox.js ===== 
import { api } from "../core/api.js"; 
import { esc } from "../core/ui.js"; 
import { navigate } from "../core/router.js"; 
import { confirmAction } from "./actions.js"; 
 
export async function render() { 
    const a = document.querySelector("#app"); 
 
   const x=await api('/api/singbox'); 
 
   if(!x)return; 
 
   /* 
    * sing-box version 原始输出包含完整构建信息。 
    * 页面这里只显示简洁的版本和运行环境简介。 
    */ 
   const versionRaw = String(x.version || ""); 
   const versionLines = versionRaw.split("\n"); 
 
   let shortVersion = "sing-box"; 
   let environment = ""; 
 
   for(const line of versionLines){ 
       const value = line.trim(); 
 
       if(value.startsWith("sing-box version")){ 
           shortVersion = value; 
       } 
 
       if(value.startsWith("Environment:")){ 
           const env = value 
               .substring("Environment:".length) 
               .trim(); 
 
           const envParts = env.split(/\s+/); 
           const parts = []; 
 
           for(const item of envParts){ 
               if(item.startsWith("go")){ 
                   parts.push(item); 
               } 
 
               if(item === "linux/arm64"){ 
                   parts.push("Linux ARM64"); 
               } 
           } 
 
           environment = parts.join(" · "); 
       } 
 
       if(value.startsWith("CGO:")){ 
           const cgo = value 
               .substring("CGO:".length) 
               .trim(); 
 
           if(cgo){ 
               environment += 
                   (environment ? " · " : "") + 
                   "CGO " + cgo; 
           } 
       } 
   } 
 
   a.innerHTML=` 
   <h2>sing-box</h2> 
 
   <div class="card"> 
 
   <h3> 
   ${esc(shortVersion)} 
   </h3> 
 
   ${ 
       environment 
       ? `<p class="muted">${esc(environment)}</p>` 
       : "" 
   } 
 
   <p> 
   配置检查： 
   <span class="${x.check_ok?'ok':'bad'}"> 
   ${x.check_ok?'通过':'失败'} 
   </span> 
   </p> 
 
   <p> 
   服务： 
   <span class="${x.active?'ok':'bad'}"> 
   ${x.active?'active':'inactive'} 
   </span> 
   </p> 
 
   <h3>当前节点</h3> 
 
   ${ 
    (x.nodes||[]).map( 
     n=>` 
     <div class="detail"> 
     <b>${esc(n.tag)}</b> 
     ${esc(n.type)} 
     · ${esc(n.port)} 
     </div> 
     ` 
    ).join('') 
   } 
 
   <h3>配置检查输出</h3> 
 
   <pre>${esc(x.check)}</pre> 
 
   <h3>TLS / 证书</h3> 
 
   <div class="card"> 
 
    <div id="cert-status"> 
     正在读取证书状态…… 
    </div> 
 
    <button 
     class="gray" 
     onclick="loadCertificateStatus()" 
    > 
     检查证书 
    </button> 
 
    <button 
     class="green" 
     onclick="issueCertificate()" 
    > 
     申请 / 续期证书 
    </button> 
 
    <p class="muted"> 
     VLESS Reality 不需要此证书。 
     此证书用于标准 TLS 节点。 
    </p> 
 
   </div> 
 
   <button onclick="restartSB()">重启 sing-box</button> 
 
   <button class="warn" 
   onclick="confirmAction('singbox-update')"> 
   更新（需确认） 
   </button> 
 
   </div> 
   `; 
 
} 
 
async function restartSB(){ 
 
 if(!confirm('确定重启 sing-box？'))return; 
 
 const x=await api( 
  '/api/singbox/restart', 
  { 
   method:'POST', 
   body:'{}' 
  } 
 ); 
 
 if(x){ 
 
  alert(JSON.stringify(x)); 
 
  navigate('singbox'); 
 
 } 
 
} 
 
 
async function loadCertificateStatus(){ 
 
 const box=document.getElementById('cert-status'); 
 
 if(!box)return; 
 
 box.textContent='正在检查……'; 
 
 const x=await api('/api/certificates'); 
 
 if(!x){ 
 
  box.textContent='读取失败'; 
 
  return; 
 
 } 
 
 let html=''; 
 
 html += '<p><b>域名：</b>'+esc(x.host||'-')+'</p>'; 
 html += '<p><b>证书：</b>'+esc(x.cert||'-')+'</p>'; 
 html += '<p><b>私钥：</b>'+esc(x.key||'-')+'</p>'; 
 
 html += '<p><b>状态：</b>'; 
 
 if(x.valid){ 
 
  html += '<span class="ok">有效</span>'; 
 
 }else{ 
 
  html += '<span class="bad">不存在或已失效</span>'; 
 
 } 
 
 html += '</p>'; 
 
 if(x.expires){ 
 
  html += '<p><b>到期：</b>'+esc(x.expires)+'</p>'; 
 
 } 
 
 if(x.days_left!==null && x.days_left!==undefined){ 
 
  html += '<p><b>剩余：</b>'+esc(String(x.days_left))+' 天</p>'; 
 
 } 
 
 if(x.issuer){ 
 
  html += '<p><b>签发者：</b>'+esc(x.issuer)+'</p>'; 
 
 } 
 
 if(x.error){ 
 
  html += '<p class="bad">'+esc(x.error)+'</p>'; 
 
 } 
 
 box.innerHTML=html; 
 
} 
 
 
 
async function issueCertificate(){ 
 
 if(!confirm( 
  '确定申请/续期 sing-box TLS 证书？\\n\\n'+ 
  '系统将使用 HTTP-01 验证。\\n'+ 
  '请确保域名 DNS 已指向本服务器且 TCP 80 可从公网访问。' 
 ))return; 
 
 const box=document.getElementById('cert-status'); 
 
 if(box)box.textContent='正在申请证书，请稍候……'; 
 
 const x=await api( 
  '/api/certificates/issue', 
  { 
   method:'POST', 
   body:JSON.stringify({ 
    confirm:'CONFIRM' 
   }) 
  } 
 ); 
 
 if(box){ 
 
  if(x && x.ok){ 
 
   box.innerHTML= 
    '<span class="ok">证书申请/续期成功</span>'; 
 
   setTimeout(loadCertificateStatus,1500); 
 
  }else{ 
 
   box.innerHTML= 
    '<span class="bad">'+ 
    esc(JSON.stringify(x))+ 
    '</span>'; 
 
  } 
 
 } 
 
}

async function ask(){

 if(!q)return; 
 
 $('#chat').innerHTML+= 
 `<div class="msg user">${esc(q)}</div>`; 
 
 $('#q').value=''; 
 
 const x=await api( 
  '/api/ai/chat', 
  { 
   method:'POST', 
   body:JSON.stringify({ 
    messages:[ 
     {role:'user',content:q} 
    ] 
   }) 
  } 
 ); 
 
 if(x){ 
 
  $('#chat').innerHTML+= 
  `<div class="msg">${esc(x.content||x.error)}</div>`; 
 
 } 
 
}

export async function render() {
    const a = document.querySelector("#app");

   const x=await api('/api/nodes');

   if(!x)return;

   const nodes=x.nodes||[];

   a.innerHTML=`
   <h2>节点管理</h2>

   <div class="card">
   当前共 <b>${nodes.length}</b> 个节点。
   <br>
   <span class="small">
   数据直接读取 /etc/sing-box/config.json，不重新生成密钥。
   </span>
   </div>

   ${
    nodes.length===0
    ?
    '<div class="card">没有发现节点。请检查 sing-box 配置。</div>'
    :
    nodes.map(n=>nodeCard(n)).join('')
   }
   `;

}

function nodeCard(n){

 let typeName={
  vless:'VLESS',
  tuic:'TUIC',
  hysteria2:'Hysteria2'
 }[n.type]||n.type;

 let protocolBadge=
  n.listen_ok
  ? '<span class="badge ok">监听正常</span>'
  : '<span class="badge bad">未检测到监听</span>';

 let tls=n.tls||{};

 let extra='';

 if(n.type==='vless'){

  extra=`
  <div class="detail">
  <b>UUID</b>
  <span>${esc(n.uuid)}</span>
  </div>

  <div class="detail">
  <b>Flow</b>
  <span>${esc(n.flow||'-')}</span>
  </div>

  <div class="detail">
  <b>SNI</b>
  <span>${esc(n.server_name||'-')}</span>
  </div>

  <div class="detail">
  <b>Reality</b>
  <span>${n.reality_enabled?'启用':'未启用'}</span>
  </div>

  ${
   n.reality_enabled
   ?
   `<div class="detail">
   <b>Short ID</b>
   <span>${esc(n.reality_short_id||'-')}</span>
   </div>`
   :
   ''
  }
  `;

 }

 if(n.type==='tuic'){

  extra=`
  <div class="detail">
  <b>UUID</b>
  <span>${esc(n.uuid)}</span>
  </div>

  <div class="detail">
  <b>Password</b>
  <span id="pw-${n.index}">••••••••</span>
  <button onclick="toggleSecret('pw-${n.index}','${esc(n.password)}')">
  显示
  </button>
  </div>

  <div class="detail">
  <b>SNI</b>
  <span>${esc(n.server_name||'-')}</span>
  </div>
  `;

 }

 if(n.type==='hysteria2'){

  extra=`
  <div class="detail">
  <b>Password</b>
  <span id="pw-${n.index}">••••••••</span>
  <button onclick="toggleSecret('pw-${n.index}','${esc(n.password)}')">
  显示
  </button>
  </div>

  <div class="detail">
  <b>SNI</b>
  <span>${esc(n.server_name||'-')}</span>
  </div>
  `;

 }

 return `
 <div class="card">

 <div class="node-title">

 <div>
 <h3>${esc(n.tag)}</h3>
 <span class="badge">${esc(typeName)}</span>
 <span class="badge">${esc(n.listen||'::')}</span>
 ${protocolBadge}
 </div>

 <div>
 <button class="gray"
 onclick="showPortEdit(${n.index},${n.port})">
 修改端口
 </button>
 </div>

 </div>

 <div class="detail-grid">

 <div class="detail">
 <b>端口</b>
 ${esc(n.port)}
 </div>

 <div class="detail">
 <b>服务</b>
 <span class="${n.active?'ok':'bad'}">
 ${n.active?'sing-box active':'sing-box inactive'}
 </span>
 </div>

 ${extra}

 </div>

 <details style="margin-top:14px">
 <summary>显示原始节点配置</summary>

 <pre>${esc(JSON.stringify(n,null,2))}</pre>

 </details>

 </div>
 `;

}


async function showPortEdit(index,current){

 const v=prompt(
  '输入新的节点端口（1-65535）',
  current
 );

 if(v===null)return;

 if(!/^[0-9]+$/.test(v)||Number(v)<1||Number(v)>65535){

  alert('端口必须是 1-65535');

  return;

 }

 if(!confirm(
  '修改节点端口 '+current+
  ' → '+v+
  '？\\n\\n系统会备份配置、检查配置并重启 sing-box。'
 ))return;

 const x=await api(
  '/api/node/port',
  {
   method:'POST',
   body:JSON.stringify({
    index:index,
    port:v,
    confirm:'CHANGE-PORT'
   })
  }
 );

 if(x){

  alert(JSON.stringify(x));

  navigate('nodes');

 }

}


function toggleSecret(id,value){

 const e=document.getElementById(id);

 if(!e)return;

 if(e.dataset.show==='1'){

  e.textContent='••••••••';
  e.dataset.show='0';

 }else{

  e.textContent=value;
  e.dataset.show='1';

 }

}


window.nodeCard = nodeCard;
window.showPortEdit = showPortEdit;
window.toggleSecret = toggleSecret;

import { api } from "../core/api.js";
import { esc } from "../core/ui.js";

export async function render() {
    const a = document.querySelector("#app");

   const x=await api('/api/subscription');

   if(!x)return;

   const url=x.url ? location.origin+x.url : '';

   const rawNodes=Array.isArray(x.raw_nodes) ? x.raw_nodes : [];

   const rawHtml=rawNodes.map(function(n){

    let label='';

    if(n.type==='vless'){
     label=n.reality ? 'VLESS Reality' : 'VLESS TLS';
    }else if(n.type==='tuic'){
     label='TUIC';
    }else if(n.type==='hysteria2'){
     label='Hysteria2';
    }else{
     label=n.type||'Node';
    }

    const realityBadge =
      n.reality
      ? '<span class="badge ok">Reality</span>'
      : '';

    const uri=String(n.uri||'');

    const safeId='raw-sub-'+String(
      n.index ?? Math.random().toString(36).slice(2)
    ).replace(/[^A-Za-z0-9_-]/g,'');

    return `
     <div class="card">

      <div class="node-title">

       <div>
        <h3>${esc(label)}</h3>
        <span class="badge">${esc(n.tag||'')}</span>
        ${realityBadge}
       </div>

       <div>
        <span class="badge">
         ${esc(String(n.port||''))}
        </span>
       </div>

      </div>

      <p class="muted">
       ${esc(n.type||'')} ·
       ${esc(n.host||'')}:
       ${esc(String(n.port||''))}
      </p>

      <textarea
       id="${safeId}"
       readonly
       rows="3"
       style="width:100%;box-sizing:border-box"
      >${esc(uri)}</textarea>

      <button
       class="green"
       onclick="copyRawNode('${safeId}',this)"
      >
       复制节点 URI
      </button>

     </div>
    `;

   }).join('');

   a.innerHTML=`

   <h2>订阅</h2>

   <div class="card">

    <h3>Clash Meta 订阅</h3>

    <p>
     Token 状态：
     <span class="${x.token_present?'ok':'bad'}">
      ${x.token_present?'已配置':'未配置'}
     </span>
    </p>

    <label>订阅地址</label>

    <input
     id="suburl"
     readonly
     value="${esc(url)}"
    >

    <button
     class="green"
     onclick="copyText('suburl')"
    >
     复制订阅地址
    </button>

    <button
     class="gray"
     onclick="openSub()"
    >
     浏览器测试
    </button>

    <p class="muted">
     订阅始终读取当前 sing-box 配置。
     节点端口修改后重新读取订阅即可获得最新端口。
    </p>

   </div>

   <h2>单节点原始订阅</h2>

   <div class="card">
    <p class="muted">
     以下 URI 直接从当前 sing-box 节点配置生成。
     URI 包含认证信息，请勿公开分享。
    </p>
   </div>

   ${
     rawHtml ||
     '<div class="card">当前没有可生成的单节点订阅。</div>'
   }

   `;

}

function copyText(id){

 const x=document.getElementById(id);

 if(!x)return;

 navigator.clipboard.writeText(x.value)
 .then(()=>alert('已复制'))
 .catch(()=>{

  x.select();
  document.execCommand('copy');
  alert('已复制');

 });

}


function openSub(){

 const x=$('#suburl');

 if(x && x.value){

  window.open(x.value,'_blank');

 }

}


function copyRawNode(id,btn){

 const x=document.getElementById(id);

 if(!x)return;

 const text=x.value||'';

 if(!text)return;

 const done=function(){

  const old=btn.textContent;

  btn.textContent='已复制';

  setTimeout(function(){

   btn.textContent=old;

  },1200);

 };

 if(navigator.clipboard && navigator.clipboard.writeText){

  navigator.clipboard.writeText(text)
   .then(done)
   .catch(function(){

    x.focus();
    x.select();

    try{
     document.execCommand('copy');
     done();
    }catch(e){}

   });

 }else{

  x.focus();
  x.select();

  try{
   document.execCommand('copy');
   done();
  }catch(e){}

 }

}



window.copyText = copyText;
window.openSub = openSub;
window.copyRawNode = copyRawNode;

import { api } from "../core/api.js";
import { $, esc, showError } from "../core/ui.js";

export async function render() {
    const a = document.querySelector("#app");

    a.innerHTML = `
    <h2>日志</h2>

    <div class="card">

    <select id="svc">
    <option>oci-linux-lab</option>
    <option>sing-box</option>
    <option>caddy</option>
    <option>ollama</option>
    </select>

    <button onclick="logs()">查看</button>

    <pre id="log">请选择服务并点击查看。</pre>

    </div>
    `;
}

async function logs() {
    try {
        const svc = $("#svc").value;

        const x = await api(
            "/api/logs?service=" + encodeURIComponent(svc)
        );

        if (x) {
            $("#log").textContent = x.log || x.error || "无日志";
        }
    } catch (e) {
        showError(e.message || String(e));
    }
}

window.logs = logs;

===== FILE: /opt/oci-zero-cost-lab/panel/static/core/api.js =====
import { showError } from "./ui.js";

let CSRF = "";

export async function api(url, opt = {}) {
    try {
        opt = {
            credentials: "same-origin",
            ...opt,
            headers: {
                ...(opt.headers || {})
            }
        };

        if (opt.method === "POST") {
            opt.headers["Content-Type"] = "application/json";

            let body = {};

            if (opt.body) {
                try {
                    body = JSON.parse(opt.body);
                } catch (e) {
                    body = {};
                }
            }

            body.csrf = CSRF;
            opt.body = JSON.stringify(body);
        }

        const r = await fetch(url, opt);

        if (r.status === 401) {
            location = "/login";
            return null;
        }

        const text = await r.text();

        let data = null;

        try {
            data = JSON.parse(text);
        } catch (e) {
            throw new Error(
                "服务器返回的不是 JSON：HTTP " + r.status
            );
        }

        if (!r.ok) {
            throw new Error(
                data.error || ("HTTP " + r.status)
            );
        }

        return data;

    } catch (e) {
        console.error(url, e);

        if (url !== "/api/session") {
            showError(e.message || String(e));
        }

        return null;
    }
}

export async function initSession() {
    const x = await api("/api/session");

    if (x && x.csrf) {
        CSRF = x.csrf;
        return true;
    }

    const y = await api("/api/status");

    if (y && y.csrf) {
        CSRF = y.csrf;
        return true;
    }

    return false;
}

export function getCSRF() {
    return CSRF;
}

===== FILE: /opt/oci-zero-cost-lab/panel/static/core/router.js =====
const routes = new Map();

export function registerRoute(name, loader) {
    routes.set(name, loader);
}

export async function navigate(name) {
    const loader = routes.get(name);

    if (!loader) {
        console.error("Unknown route:", name);
        return;
    }

    document.querySelectorAll(".nav").forEach(el => {
        el.classList.toggle(
            "on",
            el.dataset.p === name
        );
    });

    const app = document.querySelector("#app");

    if (app) {
        app.innerHTML = `
            <div class="loading">正在加载...</div>
        `;
    }

    try {
        await loader();
    } catch (error) {
        console.error("Route error:", name, error);

        if (app) {
            app.innerHTML = `
                <div class="card">
                    <div class="bad">
                        页面加载失败：${String(error)}
                    </div>
                </div>
            `;
        }
    }
}

export function show(name) {
    return navigate(name);
}

export function getRoutes() {
    return routes;
}

===== FILE: /opt/oci-zero-cost-lab/panel/static/core/ui.js =====
export function $(selector) {
    return document.querySelector(selector);
}

export function esc(value) {
    return String(value ?? "").replace(
        /[&<>"']/g,
        c => ({
            "&": "&amp;",
            "<": "&lt;",
            ">": "&gt;",
            '"': "&quot;",
            "'": "&#39;"
        }[c])
    );
}

export function showError(message) {
    const app = $("#app");

    if (app) {
        app.insertAdjacentHTML(
            "afterbegin",
            `<div class="error"><b>操作失败</b><br>${esc(message)}</div>`
        );
    }
}

===== FILE: /opt/oci-zero-cost-lab/guard/check.sh =====
#!/usr/bin/env bash
set -Eeuo pipefail
BASE="/opt/oci-zero-cost-lab"
STATE="$BASE/guard"
mkdir -p "$STATE"
URLS=(
"https://docs.oracle.com/en-us/iaas/Content/FreeTier/freetier_topic-Always_Free_Resources.htm"
"https://docs.oracle.com/iaas/Content/General/service-limits/default.htm"
"https://docs.oracle.com/iaas/Content/FreeTier/freetier.htm"
)
tmp="$(mktemp -d)"; trap 'rm -rf "$tmp"' EXIT
changed=0
for i in "${!URLS[@]}"; do
  f="$tmp/$i.html"
  if ! curl -fsSL --max-time 25 --retry 2 "${URLS[$i]}" -o "$f"; then
    echo "OCI Free Guard: failed to fetch ${URLS[$i]}" >&2
    continue
  fi
  # Keep a normalized text snapshot; don't store the full page unnecessarily.
  sed -E 's/<script[^>]*>.*<\/script>//g; s/<style[^>]*>.*<\/style>//g; s/<[^>]+>/ /g; s/[[:space:]]+/ /g' "$f" \
    | tr -d '\r' | head -c 200000 > "$STATE/page_$i.txt"
  sha="$(sha256sum "$STATE/page_$i.txt" | awk '{print $1}')"
  old="$(cat "$STATE/page_$i.sha" 2>/dev/null || true)"
  [[ "$sha" == "$old" ]] || changed=1
  printf '%s' "$sha" > "$STATE/page_$i.sha"
done
free_disk="$(df -P / | awk 'NR==2 {print $5}')"
mem="$(free | awk '/Mem:/ {printf "%.1f", $3/$2*100}')"
cpu="$(awk '{print $1}' /proc/loadavg)"
cat > "$STATE/status.json" <<EOF
{
  "checked_at": "$(date -Is)",
  "policy_changed": $([[ "$changed" -eq 1 ]] && echo true || echo false),
  "disk_root": "$free_disk",
  "memory_used_percent": "$mem",
  "load_1m": "$cpu",
  "official_sources": [
    "${URLS[0]}",
    "${URLS[1]}",
    "${URLS[2]}"
  ],
  "note": "政策变化检测基于官方页面快照哈希；它不是 OCI 计费 API 的替代品。"
}
EOF
if [[ "$changed" -eq 1 ]]; then
  printf '%s OCI official policy page changed\n' "$(date -Is)" >> "$STATE/alerts.log"
fi

}

===== FILE: /opt/oci-zero-cost-lab/panel/static/modules/nodes.js =====
import { api } from "../core/api.js";
import { esc } from "../core/ui.js";
import { navigate } from "../core/router.js";

export async function render() {
    const a = document.querySelector("#app");

   const x=await api('/api/nodes');

   if(!x)return;

   const nodes=x.nodes||[];

   a.innerHTML=`
   <h2>节点管理</h2>

   <div class="card">
   当前共 <b>${nodes.length}</b> 个节点。
   <br>
   <span class="small">
   数据直接读取 /etc/sing-box/config.json，不重新生成密钥。
   </span>
   </div>

   ${
    nodes.length===0
    ?
    '<div class="card">没有发现节点。请检查 sing-box 配置。</div>'
    :
    nodes.map(n=>nodeCard(n)).join('')
   }
   `;

}

function nodeCard(n){

 let typeName={
  vless:'VLESS',
  tuic:'TUIC',
  hysteria2:'Hysteria2'
 }[n.type]||n.type;

 let protocolBadge=
  n.listen_ok
  ? '<span class="badge ok">监听正常</span>'
  : '<span class="badge bad">未检测到监听</span>';

 let tls=n.tls||{};

 let extra='';

 if(n.type==='vless'){

  extra=`
  <div class="detail">
  <b>UUID</b>
  <span>${esc(n.uuid)}</span>
  </div>

  <div class="detail">
  <b>Flow</b>
  <span>${esc(n.flow||'-')}</span>
  </div>

  <div class="detail">
  <b>SNI</b>
  <span>${esc(n.server_name||'-')}</span>
  </div>

  <div class="detail">
  <b>Reality</b>
  <span>${n.reality_enabled?'启用':'未启用'}</span>
  </div>

  ${
   n.reality_enabled
   ?
   `<div class="detail">
   <b>Short ID</b>
   <span>${esc(n.reality_short_id||'-')}</span>
   </div>`
   :
   ''
  }
  `;

 }

 if(n.type==='tuic'){

  extra=`
  <div class="detail">
  <b>UUID</b>
  <span>${esc(n.uuid)}</span>
  </div>

  <div class="detail">
  <b>Password</b>
  <span id="pw-${n.index}">••••••••</span>
  <button onclick="toggleSecret('pw-${n.index}','${esc(n.password)}')">
  显示
  </button>
  </div>

  <div class="detail">
  <b>SNI</b>
  <span>${esc(n.server_name||'-')}</span>
  </div>
  `;

 }

 if(n.type==='hysteria2'){

  extra=`
  <div class="detail">
  <b>Password</b>
  <span id="pw-${n.index}">••••••••</span>
  <button onclick="toggleSecret('pw-${n.index}','${esc(n.password)}')">
  显示
  </button>
  </div>

  <div class="detail">
  <b>SNI</b>
  <span>${esc(n.server_name||'-')}</span>
  </div>
  `;

 }

 return `
 <div class="card">

 <div class="node-title">

 <div>
 <h3>${esc(n.tag)}</h3>
 <span class="badge">${esc(typeName)}</span>
 <span class="badge">${esc(n.listen||'::')}</span>
 ${protocolBadge}
 </div>

 <div>
 <button class="gray"
 onclick="showPortEdit(${n.index},${n.port})">
 修改端口
 </button>
 </div>

 </div>

 <div class="detail-grid">

 <div class="detail">
 <b>端口</b>
 ${esc(n.port)}
 </div>

 <div class="detail">
 <b>服务</b>
 <span class="${n.active?'ok':'bad'}">
 ${n.active?'sing-box active':'sing-box inactive'}
 </span>
 </div>

 ${extra}

 </div>

 <details style="margin-top:14px">
 <summary>显示原始节点配置</summary>

 <pre>${esc(JSON.stringify(n,null,2))}</pre>

 </details>

 </div>
 `;

}


async function showPortEdit(index,current){

 const v=prompt(
  '输入新的节点端口（1-65535）',
  current
 );

 if(v===null)return;

 if(!/^[0-9]+$/.test(v)||Number(v)<1||Number(v)>65535){

  alert('端口必须是 1-65535');

  return;

 }

 if(!confirm(
  '修改节点端口 '+current+
  ' → '+v+
  '？\\n\\n系统会备份配置、检查配置并重启 sing-box。'
 ))return;

 const x=await api(
  '/api/node/port',
  {
   method:'POST',
   body:JSON.stringify({
    index:index,
    port:v,
    confirm:'CHANGE-PORT'
   })
  }
 );

 if(x){

  alert(JSON.stringify(x));

  navigate('nodes');

 }

}


function toggleSecret(id,value){

 const e=document.getElementById(id);

 if(!e)return;

 if(e.dataset.show==='1'){

  e.textContent='••••••••';
  e.dataset.show='0';

 }else{

  e.textContent=value;
  e.dataset.show='1';

 }

}


window.nodeCard = nodeCard;
window.showPortEdit = showPortEdit;
window.toggleSecret = toggleSecret;

import { api } from "../core/api.js";
import { esc } from "../core/ui.js";

export async function render() {
    const a = document.querySelector("#app");

   const x=await api('/api/subscription');

   if(!x)return;

   const url=x.url ? location.origin+x.url : '';

   const rawNodes=Array.isArray(x.raw_nodes) ? x.raw_nodes : [];

   const rawHtml=rawNodes.map(function(n){

    let label='';

    if(n.type==='vless'){
     label=n.reality ? 'VLESS Reality' : 'VLESS TLS';
    }else if(n.type==='tuic'){
     label='TUIC';
    }else if(n.type==='hysteria2'){
     label='Hysteria2';
    }else{
     label=n.type||'Node';
    }

    const realityBadge =
      n.reality
      ? '<span class="badge ok">Reality</span>'
      : '';

    const uri=String(n.uri||'');

    const safeId='raw-sub-'+String(
      n.index ?? Math.random().toString(36).slice(2)
    ).replace(/[^A-Za-z0-9_-]/g,'');

    return `
     <div class="card">

      <div class="node-title">

       <div>
        <h3>${esc(label)}</h3>
        <span class="badge">${esc(n.tag||'')}</span>
        ${realityBadge}
       </div>

       <div>
        <span class="badge">
         ${esc(String(n.port||''))}
        </span>
       </div>

      </div>

      <p class="muted">
       ${esc(n.type||'')} ·
       ${esc(n.host||'')}:
       ${esc(String(n.port||''))}
      </p>

      <textarea
       id="${safeId}"
       readonly
       rows="3"
       style="width:100%;box-sizing:border-box"
      >${esc(uri)}</textarea>

      <button
       class="green"
       onclick="copyRawNode('${safeId}',this)"
      >
       复制节点 URI
      </button>

     </div>
    `;

   }).join('');

   a.innerHTML=`

   <h2>订阅</h2>

   <div class="card">

    <h3>Clash Meta 订阅</h3>

    <p>
     Token 状态：
     <span class="${x.token_present?'ok':'bad'}">
      ${x.token_present?'已配置':'未配置'}
     </span>
    </p>

    <label>订阅地址</label>

    <input
     id="suburl"
     readonly
     value="${esc(url)}"
    >

    <button
     class="green"
     onclick="copyText('suburl')"
    >
     复制订阅地址
    </button>

    <button
     class="gray"
     onclick="openSub()"
    >
     浏览器测试
    </button>

    <p class="muted">
     订阅始终读取当前 sing-box 配置。
     节点端口修改后重新读取订阅即可获得最新端口。
    </p>

   </div>

   <h2>单节点原始订阅</h2>

   <div class="card">
    <p class="muted">
     以下 URI 直接从当前 sing-box 节点配置生成。
     URI 包含认证信息，请勿公开分享。
    </p>
   </div>

   ${
     rawHtml ||
     '<div class="card">当前没有可生成的单节点订阅。</div>'
   }

   `;

}

function copyText(id){

 const x=document.getElementById(id);

 if(!x)return;

 navigator.clipboard.writeText(x.value)
 .then(()=>alert('已复制'))
 .catch(()=>{

  x.select();
  document.execCommand('copy');
  alert('已复制');

 });

}


function openSub(){

 const x=$('#suburl');

 if(x && x.value){

  window.open(x.value,'_blank');

 }

}


function copyRawNode(id,btn){

 const x=document.getElementById(id);

 if(!x)return;

 const text=x.value||'';

 if(!text)return;

 const done=function(){

  const old=btn.textContent;

  btn.textContent='已复制';

  setTimeout(function(){

   btn.textContent=old;

  },1200);

 };

 if(navigator.clipboard && navigator.clipboard.writeText){

  navigator.clipboard.writeText(text)
   .then(done)
   .catch(function(){

    x.focus();
    x.select();

    try{
     document.execCommand('copy');
     done();
    }catch(e){}

   });

 }else{

  x.focus();
  x.select();

  try{
   document.execCommand('copy');
   done();
  }catch(e){}

 }

}



window.copyText = copyText;
window.openSub = openSub;
window.copyRawNode = copyRawNode;

import { api } from "../core/api.js";
import { $, esc, showError } from "../core/ui.js";

export async function render() {
    const a = document.querySelector("#app");

    a.innerHTML = `
    <h2>日志</h2>

    <div class="card">

    <select id="svc">
    <option>oci-linux-lab</option>
    <option>sing-box</option>
    <option>caddy</option>
    <option>ollama</option>
    </select>

    <button onclick="logs()">查看</button>

    <pre id="log">请选择服务并点击查看。</pre>

    </div>
    `;
}

async function logs() {
    try {
        const svc = $("#svc").value;

        const x = await api(
            "/api/logs?service=" + encodeURIComponent(svc)
        );

        if (x) {
            $("#log").textContent = x.log || x.error || "无日志";
        }
    } catch (e) {
        showError(e.message || String(e));
    }
}

window.logs = logs;

===== FILE: /opt/oci-zero-cost-lab/panel/static/core/api.js =====
import { showError } from "./ui.js";

let CSRF = "";

export async function api(url, opt = {}) {
    try {
        opt = {
            credentials: "same-origin",
            ...opt,
            headers: {
                ...(opt.headers || {})
            }
        };

        if (opt.method === "POST") {
            opt.headers["Content-Type"] = "application/json";

            let body = {};

            if (opt.body) {
                try {
                    body = JSON.parse(opt.body);
                } catch (e) {
                    body = {};
                }
            }

            body.csrf = CSRF;
            opt.body = JSON.stringify(body);
        }

        const r = await fetch(url, opt);

        if (r.status === 401) {
            location = "/login";
            return null;
        }

        const text = await r.text();

        let data = null;

        try {
            data = JSON.parse(text);
        } catch (e) {
            throw new Error(
                "服务器返回的不是 JSON：HTTP " + r.status
            );
        }

        if (!r.ok) {
            throw new Error(
                data.error || ("HTTP " + r.status)
            );
        }

        return data;

    } catch (e) {
        console.error(url, e);

        if (url !== "/api/session") {
            showError(e.message || String(e));
        }

        return null;
    }
}

export async function initSession() {
    const x = await api("/api/session");

    if (x && x.csrf) {
        CSRF = x.csrf;
        return true;
    }

    const y = await api("/api/status");

    if (y && y.csrf) {
        CSRF = y.csrf;
        return true;
    }

    return false;
}

export function getCSRF() {
    return CSRF;
}

===== FILE: /opt/oci-zero-cost-lab/panel/static/core/router.js =====
const routes = new Map();

export function registerRoute(name, loader) {
    routes.set(name, loader);
}

export async function navigate(name) {
    const loader = routes.get(name);

    if (!loader) {
        console.error("Unknown route:", name);
        return;
    }

    document.querySelectorAll(".nav").forEach(el => {
        el.classList.toggle(
            "on",
            el.dataset.p === name
        );
    });

    const app = document.querySelector("#app");

    if (app) {
        app.innerHTML = `
            <div class="loading">正在加载...</div>
        `;
    }

    try {
        await loader();
    } catch (error) {
        console.error("Route error:", name, error);

        if (app) {
            app.innerHTML = `
                <div class="card">
                    <div class="bad">
                        页面加载失败：${String(error)}
                    </div>
                </div>
            `;
        }
    }
}

export function show(name) {
    return navigate(name);
}

export function getRoutes() {
    return routes;
}

===== FILE: /opt/oci-zero-cost-lab/panel/static/core/ui.js =====
export function $(selector) {
    return document.querySelector(selector);
}

export function esc(value) {
    return String(value ?? "").replace(
        /[&<>"']/g,
        c => ({
            "&": "&amp;",
            "<": "&lt;",
            ">": "&gt;",
            '"': "&quot;",
            "'": "&#39;"
        }[c])
    );
}

export function showError(message) {
    const app = $("#app");

    if (app) {
        app.insertAdjacentHTML(
            "afterbegin",
            `<div class="error"><b>操作失败</b><br>${esc(message)}</div>`
        );
    }
}

===== FILE: /opt/oci-zero-cost-lab/guard/check.sh =====
#!/usr/bin/env bash
set -Eeuo pipefail
BASE="/opt/oci-zero-cost-lab"
STATE="$BASE/guard"
mkdir -p "$STATE"
URLS=(
"https://docs.oracle.com/en-us/iaas/Content/FreeTier/freetier_topic-Always_Free_Resources.htm"
"https://docs.oracle.com/iaas/Content/General/service-limits/default.htm"
"https://docs.oracle.com/iaas/Content/FreeTier/freetier.htm"
)
tmp="$(mktemp -d)"; trap 'rm -rf "$tmp"' EXIT
changed=0
for i in "${!URLS[@]}"; do
  f="$tmp/$i.html"
  if ! curl -fsSL --max-time 25 --retry 2 "${URLS[$i]}" -o "$f"; then
    echo "OCI Free Guard: failed to fetch ${URLS[$i]}" >&2
    continue
  fi
  # Keep a normalized text snapshot; don't store the full page unnecessarily.
  sed -E 's/<script[^>]*>.*<\/script>//g; s/<style[^>]*>.*<\/style>//g; s/<[^>]+>/ /g; s/[[:space:]]+/ /g' "$f" \
    | tr -d '\r' | head -c 200000 > "$STATE/page_$i.txt"
  sha="$(sha256sum "$STATE/page_$i.txt" | awk '{print $1}')"
  old="$(cat "$STATE/page_$i.sha" 2>/dev/null || true)"
  [[ "$sha" == "$old" ]] || changed=1
  printf '%s' "$sha" > "$STATE/page_$i.sha"
done
free_disk="$(df -P / | awk 'NR==2 {print $5}')"
mem="$(free | awk '/Mem:/ {printf "%.1f", $3/$2*100}')"
cpu="$(awk '{print $1}' /proc/loadavg)"
cat > "$STATE/status.json" <<EOF
{
  "checked_at": "$(date -Is)",
  "policy_changed": $([[ "$changed" -eq 1 ]] && echo true || echo false),
  "disk_root": "$free_disk",
  "memory_used_percent": "$mem",
  "load_1m": "$cpu",
  "official_sources": [
    "${URLS[0]}",
    "${URLS[1]}",
    "${URLS[2]}"
  ],
  "note": "政策变化检测基于官方页面快照哈希；它不是 OCI 计费 API 的替代品。"
}
EOF
if [[ "$changed" -eq 1 ]]; then
  printf '%s OCI official policy page changed\n' "$(date -Is)" >> "$STATE/alerts.log"
fi

}

ExecStart=/opt/oci-zero-cost-lab/guard/check.sh
[exit=0]

NoNewPrivileges=true
ProtectSystem=strict
ProtectHome=true
PrivateTmp=true
PrivateDevices=true
ProtectKernelTunables=true
ProtectKernelModules=true

RestrictSUIDSGID=true
RestrictRealtime=true

MemoryDenyWriteExecute=false
